# Skills · C · part 23

814 entries, cbt-chaos-issue-triage to cc-srs-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cbt-chaos-issue-triage**: Triage a CBT Chaos v2 test by reading its Jira issue under cbt-chaos/scenarios_v2/&lt;scenario_id&gt;/jira-issue.md, checking the actual cluster and the active virt-cbt-lab workflow, validating the chaos injection against the live Krkn registry, and generating a detailed scenario-spec.md plus a minimal chaos-trigger.sh. Use for designing or preparing a v2 scenario; do not execute E2E or inject chaos unless separately requested. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-issue-triage))
- **cbt-chaos-krkn-preparer**: CBT chaos team design-time specialist that turns a disruption intent into a validated krknctl command: loads the krkn-scenario skill for flag accuracy, queries live krknctl metadata, builds the command, validates event-trigger feasibility for the injection window, dry-runs the exact argv, and returns compact evidence — or a justified scoped oc/kubectl fallback when Krkn cannot express the action. Used at triage (new scenarios) and improvement (trigger corrections); never mid-run, never executes the disruption live. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-krkn-preparer))
- **cbt-chaos-lead-v2**: Own one CBT chaos scenario end to end as the dedicated Lead subagent: prepare through specialists, relay human approval through main, execute the approved bundle, obtain independent grading, report and verify local closeout. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-lead-v2))
- **cbt-chaos-observer-worker**: Long-lived read-only CBT chaos team worker and the run's independent injection witness. Arms scoped Kubernetes watches (ARMED), preserves terminal evidence before recovery (PRESERVED), delivers a bounded final analysis (FINAL), and keeps raw streams on disk. Exactly one per run; never mutates, never grades. Used when the Lead spawns the Observer Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/observer.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-observer-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-preflight-worker**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that validates one scenario run before any live execution: spec/trigger/cluster agreement, trigger script static checks, Krkn dry-run, cluster readiness, and destination PVC capacity headroom. Returns GO or NO-GO only; never mutates anything. Used when the Lead spawns the Preflight Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/preflight.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-preflight-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-recovery-worker**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that runs the post-chaos same-VM recovery validation: new full/incremental backup on the recovered original VM with fresh object/PVC names, tracker/checkpoint checks, and restore verification. Acts only on Lead authorization per action, after PRESERVED. Used when the Lead spawns the Recovery Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/recovery.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-recovery-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-report-checker**: Blind CBT chaos team checker that grades report fidelity: the two run reports must be standalone-readable, contain only observed facts, cite real evidence, and contain no harness internals, reconstructed commands, or expectations stated as facts. Returns PASS or REJECT (revision loop). Used when the Lead spawns the Report Fidelity Checker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/check-report.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-report-checker))
- **cbt-chaos-report-writer**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that writes the two engineer-facing run reports (test-run-result.md, test-run-report.md) strictly from the closed evidence pack. Standalone, observed-facts-only, no harness internals. Used when the Lead spawns the Report Writer with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/report-writer.md after the verdict is assigned. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-report-writer))
- **cbt-chaos-run**: Run a KubeVirt CBT chaos scenario with product-focused full/incremental backup, checkpoint/tracker, CBT recovery, and restore verification. Validate scenario execution separately from the product result and report harness findings outside the standalone result/report. Supports v1 and CBT v2 scenarios; use when a user requests a scenario run or report. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-run))
- **cbt-chaos-run-analyst**: Post-run CBT chaos team agent that analyzes one closed run: UTC timeline linking every stage, criterion-by-criterion table, product-vs-harness finding separation, and contradictions/confidence limits. Reads only the current run's closed artifacts. Analysis only — proposes nothing live. Used when the Lead spawns the Run Analyst after the run reaches closed. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-run-analyst))
- **cbt-chaos-scenario-improver**: Post-run CBT chaos team agent that finds weaknesses in the test itself — spec, trigger, observer coverage, checker gaps, capacity planning — and proposes concrete diffs plus harness-ticket drafts. Trigger-script diffs are approved and applied by the Lead between runs; spec and SKILL.md diffs always require the human. Improves the scenario and harness, not the CBT product. Used when the Lead spawns the Scenario Improver after the run reaches closed. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-scenario-improver))
- **cbt-chaos-simple**: Execute one CBT chaos scenario simply and directly on the fixed execution host `ssh cloud05` (checkout /root/v2/virt-cbt-lab, kubeconfig /root/mno/kubeconfig — all make/oc/krknctl commands run there). Takes a scenario ID (e.g. CBT-F-08), reads its definition under cbt-chaos/scenarios_v2/&lt;ID&gt;/, plans the run, uses the krkn-scenario skill to derive and validate the krknctl command (or drafts an oc/kubectl fallback for faults krknctl cannot express), discusses the injection approach with the user, prechecks it, runs the make pipeline stage with the injector armed, validates the outcome through t… ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-simple))
- **cbt-chaos-team**: Orchestrate a team of subagents for a CBT Chaos scenario run: one Lead agent that manages the run, plans and starts short-lived Workers, keeps the per-run state files, and reconciles the verdict; blind Checkers that independently pass or reject injection validity, evidence sufficiency, and report fidelity; and post-run Analyst/Improver agents that propose scenario, harness, and CBT-product improvements plus a bounded re-run matrix. Delegates the live run protocol (phases, safety, evidence rules) to the cbt-chaos-run skill; this skill adds the team topology, role contracts, and per-run state f… ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-team))
- **cbt-chaos-team-v2**: Dispatch one CBT chaos scenario to the dedicated Sol Lead subagent. Main relays human approval and clarification requests; Lead owns preparation, execution, independent grading coordination, reporting and verified closeout. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-team-v2))
- **cbt-chaos-trigger-preparer-v2**: Chaos Engineer for CBT chaos v2: author/revise the scenario bundle as a bundle-preparer, or complete one bounded prep/investigation/draft task as a task-specialist. Prefer native krknctl event triggers, then wrapped krknctl; use oc/kubectl only for faults krknctl cannot express, and only after explicit user consent. Never execute a disruption or grade your own work. Spawned by cbt-chaos-lead-v2; not user-invocable directly. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cbt-chaos-trigger-preparer-v2))
- **cbt-chaos-trigger-worker**: Short-lived CBT chaos team worker that executes the verified chaos trigger for one run over SSH from the configured remote checkout, preserves separate workflow and action exit codes, and records the UTC action request with exact target identity. Gated on watch health at the injection boundary. Used when the Lead spawns the Trigger Worker with runs/&lt;RUN_ID&gt;/contracts/trigger.md. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-trigger-worker))
- **cbt-chaos-variation-decider**: Post-run CBT chaos team agent that decides whether to re-run the same scenario with different parameters and proposes the next bounded matrix leg: dimensions are backup mode (Full/Incremental), injection window position, storage backend, workload sizing, and OS. Proposes at most 4 legs per approved matrix, with hard stop conditions. Proposal only; the Lead executes and the human approves. Used when the Lead spawns the Variation Decider after the run reaches closed. ([ddjain/virt-cbt-lab](https://github.com/ddjain/virt-cbt-lab/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills-archive/cbt-chaos-variation-decider))

- **cbt-thought-record**: Crée une fiche TCC pour examiner une pensée difficile, sans remplacer une psychothérapie. À utiliser pour ruminations, pensées automatiques, peur du jugement, culpabilité ou catastrophisme. Se déclenche aussi avec "je n'arrête pas de penser que…", "j'ai peur que…", "je me sens coupable", "et si tout allait mal", "je rumine", ou toute pensée négative répétitive. Also triggers on "CBT thought record", "challenge a negative thought", "cognitive distortions". ([vantaflow/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/vantaflow/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/psy-skills/cbt-thought-record) · ★30 · 2 copies)

- **CBT中间信念检视与更新工作流程**: 运用苏格拉底式提问、成本-效益分析等技术，协助来访者检视其条件化假设（如'如果…就…'），发展更具适应性的中间信念，适用于情绪改善后需巩固认知弹性的情境。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E4%B8%AD%E9%97%B4%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E6%A3%80%E8%A7%86%E4%B8%8E%E6%9B%B4%E6%96%B0%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT中间信念矫正工作表引导**: 在CBT咨询第三阶段，当来访者已建立基本治疗联盟并识别出适应不良中间信念时，使用结构化工作表引导其通过苏格拉底式提问与现实检验完成信念重构。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E4%B8%AD%E9%97%B4%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E7%9F%AB%E6%AD%A3%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E8%A1%A8%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT五栏表家庭作业引导**: 结构化引导来访者在真实生活情境中完成五栏表（触发事件—自动思维—情绪/生理反应—支持与反驳证据—基于证据的替代思维），以建立元认知监控与认知重评能力；须嵌入苏格拉底式自我提问与微型行为试验设计，确保替代思维具实证基础与可操作性。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E4%BA%94%E6%A0%8F%E8%A1%A8%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT五阶段结构化咨询流程**: 将CBT咨询划分为五个递进阶段（关系建立与风险评估、创伤回溯、CBT原理教学与自动思维识别、支持系统建设与信念重构、自我悦纳与自信重塑），每阶段明确目标、时长与核心任务，嵌入学校系统协作节点（如专项心理帮扶基金、医院绿色通道），支持跨会话节奏控制与阶段跃迁。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E4%BA%94%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT修通阶段三栏表+产婆术式辩论组合**: 在生活事件语境中，协助来访者识别非黑即白、以偏概全、糟糕至极、无端否认、以己度人、管状视野等六类典型不合理思维，并用更理性、可观察、可验证、可行动的想法替代，从而调节情绪反应。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E4%BF%AE%E9%80%9A%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E4%B8%89%E6%A0%8F%E8%A1%A8%2B%E4%BA%A7%E5%A9%86%E6%9C%AF%E5%BC%8F%E8%BE%A9%E8%AE%BA%E7%BB%84%E5%90%88) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT六次结构化咨询流程**: 一个标准化的6次CBT短期咨询框架，覆盖初始评估、自动思维干预、中间信念重构、核心信念探索、家庭系统觉察与结束巩固六个阶段。适用于轻中度情绪障碍青少年个案的学校心理咨询场景。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%85%AD%E6%AC%A1%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT六步结构化评估流程**: 在CBT初始评估阶段，按六个固定步骤系统收集信息：问题界定、影响因素识别、目标澄清、基线数据采集、咨询同盟共建、方案制定。用于首次或阶段性再评估。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%85%AD%E6%AD%A5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT初始会谈：个案概念化与治疗联盟建立**: 在前1–2次CBT会谈中，系统完成发展性与系统性信息采集，通过心境检查与行为功能分析形成可操作的个案概念化，并与患者及家属共同澄清结构化设置、开展心理教育、共建SMART目标清单，从而确立稳固的治疗联盟。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%88%9D%E5%A7%8B%E4%BC%9A%E8%B0%88%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%AA%E6%A1%88%E6%A6%82%E5%BF%B5%E5%8C%96%E4%B8%8E%E6%B2%BB%E7%96%97%E8%81%94%E7%9B%9F%E5%BB%BA%E7%AB%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT初始关系建立与评估双轨流程**: 在第1–2次咨询中同步开展咨询关系建立（倾听、共情、无条件积极关注）与结构化诊断评估（开放性提问收集资料、鉴别诊断、衔接医生诊断），为后续CBT干预锚定方向。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%88%9D%E5%A7%8B%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%E5%BB%BA%E7%AB%8B%E4%B8%8E%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0%E5%8F%8C%E8%BD%A8%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT初始联盟建构三步法**: 在认知行为治疗前3次咨询中，系统建立咨询联盟：共情接纳降低羞耻感、隐喻式心理教育增强信心、共同制定可操作目标。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%88%9D%E5%A7%8B%E8%81%94%E7%9B%9F%E5%BB%BA%E6%9E%84%E4%B8%89%E6%AD%A5%E6%B3%95) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT呼吸放松训练标准化脚本**: 指导来访者执行4-1-6秒深慢呼吸（吸气4秒-屏息1秒-呼气6秒），配合身体扫描与意象引导，每日2次×5分钟，用于即时缓解恐惧相关的自主神经激活。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%91%BC%E5%90%B8%E6%94%BE%E6%9D%BE%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%E5%8C%96%E8%84%9A%E6%9C%AC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT咨询末期结构化回顾与强化**: 在CBT咨询末期（第20–22次），系统回顾问题改善进程，将改变归因于来访者自身，并结合CBT原理反复强化认知行为改变机制；使用视觉化描述呈现波动性进展，建立对复发的现实预期。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%9C%AB%E6%9C%9F%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%9B%9E%E9%A1%BE%E4%B8%8E%E5%BC%BA%E5%8C%96) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT咨询末期结构化回顾与强化**: 在CBT咨询末期（第20–22次），系统回顾来访者问题改善进程，将具体改变经验与CBT核心原理（如认知重构、行为实验、双重故事）联结；通过视觉化描述（如波动曲线）正常化进展中的停滞、反复与暂时性倒退；强调改变主因在于来访者自身能动性，并引导其内化并承诺持续使用已掌握的CBT工具。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E5%90%8E%E7%8E%B0%E4%BB%A3%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%9C%AB%E6%9C%9F%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%9B%9E%E9%A1%BE%E4%B8%8E%E5%BC%BA%E5%8C%96) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT咨询终止阶段结构化回顾与成长确认**: 在CBT咨询末期，系统回顾咨询过程、进展与来访者自我觉察提升，强化其对自身成长的确认，并为后续自主探索做准备。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E7%BB%88%E6%AD%A2%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%9B%9E%E9%A1%BE%E4%B8%8E%E6%88%90%E9%95%BF%E7%A1%AE%E8%AE%A4) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT咨询结束期四段式整合回顾**: 在最后一次CBT咨询中，以结构化、来访者中心的方式引导其自主复述问题起源、改变过程、当前状态与未来应用，强化认知行为策略的生活迁移意识，完成自然、有准备的咨询结束。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E7%BB%93%E6%9D%9F%E6%9C%9F%E5%9B%9B%E6%AE%B5%E5%BC%8F%E6%95%B4%E5%90%88%E5%9B%9E%E9%A1%BE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT四阶段咨询框架**: 一个结构化的认知行为疗法宏观协议，将咨询划分为关系建立与概念化、认知重构、行为干预、效果巩固与结束四个递进阶段。适用于强迫症等焦虑相关障碍的系统性CBT干预。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT四阶段咨询结构化流程**: 将CBT咨询划分为开始、认知重建、信念矫正与结束四个阶段的跨-session 宏观协议，规定各阶段目标、时长范围与核心任务。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT四阶段结构化咨询协议**: 一个跨会话的、阶段化的CBT宏观治疗流程，包含心理诊断、领悟、修通与再教育四个递进阶段，用于系统性开展大学新生适应不良的短期认知行为干预。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT四阶段结构化咨询流程**: 将CBT咨询划分为心理评估、关系建立与家庭关系梳理、不合理认知重构、人际关系泛化四个递进阶段，每阶段含目标、方法、过程和作业布置；阶段间以一周间隔为观察窗口，次数弹性（2–3次/阶段），依据家庭关系改善程度与认知调整进展动态调整。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT四阶段结构化治疗流程**: 一个跨阶段、多会话的CBT治疗宏观框架，按问题理解→概念化与目标设定→认知行为干预→巩固与预防复发四阶段组织10次住院心理治疗。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%B2%BB%E7%96%97%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT复发应对卡制作**: 指导来访者制作个性化应对卡，明确写出咨询结束后若问题反复时的具体应对步骤、潜在阻碍识别及资源调用方式，强化其独立解决问题的能力与掌控感。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%91%E5%BA%94%E5%AF%B9%E5%8D%A1%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT复发预防与家庭协同巩固协议**: 在治疗终期（第8–9次）结构化会谈中，同步开展个体技能巩固与家庭系统干预：引导患者归因进步、复习技术、建立自我效能；联合家庭成员强化情感联结、澄清出院担忧、学习运用CBT技术应对日常问题，并共同制定可操作的复发预警与响应计划。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%91%E9%A2%84%E9%98%B2%E4%B8%8E%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E5%8D%8F%E5%90%8C%E5%B7%A9%E5%9B%BA%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT复发预防结构化收尾**: 在咨询结束期（倒数2–3次），系统引导来访者结构化回顾CBT学习历程，将行为/情绪改善显性联结至认知重构与行为实验原理，协同共建具象化、可观察的个性化复发征兆信号清单，并分层制定三级应对策略（自我调节→支持系统启动→专业求助触发），最终锚定一项SMART原则校验通过的未来90天行动目标，以巩固疗效、增强自我管理能力、降低复发风险。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%91%E9%A2%84%E9%98%B2%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%94%B6%E5%B0%BE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT家庭作业布置**: 引导来访者识别不合理信念，按'更灵活、更现实、更具适应性'三原则共同构建可操作的合理信念，并书面记录。用于巩固认知重构效果，促进行为迁移。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%B8%83%E7%BD%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT家庭作业布置与跟进**: 在CBT咨询中，系统性布置并结构化跟进家庭作业，以促进认知重构与行为改变的泛化。适用于已建立工作联盟、完成初步评估、进入正式干预阶段的来访者。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%B8%83%E7%BD%AE%E4%B8%8E%E8%B7%9F%E8%BF%9B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT家庭作业结构化布置**: 在咨询末尾，针对当次核心认知主题，布置具身化、可记录、可复盘的简明作业，聚焦‘觉察—记录—归因’三要素，为下一次认知工作提供实证素材。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%B8%83%E7%BD%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT导向的复发预防应对卡制作**: 指导来访者在咨询结束阶段制作个性化‘应对卡’，以结构化方式明确问题反复时的具体行动步骤、潜在阻碍识别及替代应对策略，从而具象化复发预期、降低不确定性焦虑，并锚定其已掌握的CBT能力资源。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E5%90%8E%E7%8E%B0%E4%BB%A3%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AF%BC%E5%90%91%E7%9A%84%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%91%E9%A2%84%E9%98%B2%E5%BA%94%E5%AF%B9%E5%8D%A1%E5%88%B6%E4%BD%9C) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT导向的焦虑状态初步评估协议**: 在首次1–2次咨询中，整合共情、积极关注与结构化问题澄清，快速聚焦就业压力、失眠、忧虑等核心症状群，形成‘焦虑状态’工作诊断，为后续CBT干预提供靶点依据。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AF%BC%E5%90%91%E7%9A%84%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E7%8A%B6%E6%80%81%E5%88%9D%E6%AD%A5%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT导向的认知重构引导对话**: 在建立基本信任后，聚焦识别并挑战学生持有的核心消极自动思维（如‘人生已经烂透了’），通过苏格拉底式提问、证据检验、认知扭曲命名与替代性想法生成等CBT技术，促使其检验思维真实性并发展更平衡、具实证基础的认知灵活性。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AF%BC%E5%90%91%E7%9A%84%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E9%87%8D%E6%9E%84%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC%E5%AF%B9%E8%AF%9D) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT导向的阶段性咨询方案制定协议**: 在第二次咨询中，基于心理测验结果与日常观察，协同求助者共同制定含近期目标与远期目标的咨询计划，并明确以合理情绪疗法为主、系统脱敏与放松疗法为辅的方法组合。适用于已完成初评、需锚定干预方向且具备目标协商能力的个案。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%AF%BC%E5%90%91%E7%9A%84%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E6%80%A7%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%96%B9%E6%A1%88%E5%88%B6%E5%AE%9A%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT就业焦虑三阶段咨询框架**: 一个结构化、时间限定的三阶段CBT干预流程，专用于大学生就业焦虑问题，覆盖认知重构、行为激活与巩固结束三个核心阶段。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%B0%B1%E4%B8%9A%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E4%B8%89%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT工具移交与泛化训练**: 在咨询终止阶段，系统性地将CBT核心工具（三栏表、思维记录表、箭头向下技术、行为实验、双重故事）移交来访者，引导其识别适用情境，鼓励持续收集支持新信念（如‘我有我价值’‘我有能力’）的实证，并付诸行动，以实现技术自主迁移与日常泛化。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7%E7%A7%BB%E4%BA%A4%E4%B8%8E%E6%B3%9B%E5%8C%96%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT巩固与防复发阶段结构化流程**: 在咨询末期（第11–12次）系统开展效果巩固、技能迁移与复发预防的标准化会话流程，适用于已完成核心CBT干预且症状显著缓解的来访者。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%B7%A9%E5%9B%BA%E4%B8%8E%E9%98%B2%E5%A4%8D%E5%8F%91%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT强迫症三阶段短程干预框架**: 结构化8次CBT咨询流程，按评估性会谈→咨询性会谈→巩固性会谈三阶段推进，整合悬搁接地技术与认知行为策略，专用于强迫症伴轻度抑郁状态、具自省力与作业执行力的成年来访者。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E5%90%8E%E7%8E%B0%E4%BB%A3%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%BC%BA%E8%BF%AB%E7%97%87%E4%B8%89%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E7%9F%AD%E7%A8%8B%E5%B9%B2%E9%A2%84%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT心境检查与认知三角心理教育**: 在每次咨询起始进行心境评分与情绪命名，并同步引入认知三角模型（情境-想法-情绪-行为），作为心理教育锚点，帮助来访者建立对认知-情绪关联的元认知觉察。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E5%BF%83%E5%A2%83%E6%A3%80%E6%9F%A5%E4%B8%8E%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E4%B8%89%E8%A7%92%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT思维-情绪关系 psychoeducation**: 向来访者简明解释认知行为疗法中事件、思维与情绪三者之间的因果关系，帮助其建立初步的元认知觉察框架。用于咨询初期建立治疗联盟和心理教育基础。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4-%E6%83%85%E7%BB%AA%E5%85%B3%E7%B3%BB%20psychoeducation) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT技能归因与生活化迁移训练**: 在咨询结束阶段，将来访者在咨询中习得并实践的CBT工具（如三栏表、箭头向下、双重故事）明确归因于其自身能动性与选择，并协同其识别72小时内可及的真实生活情境，设定微小、可验证的行为证据收集任务，以支持新核心信念（如‘我有我价值’‘我有能力’）的持续内化与行动化。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E5%90%8E%E7%8E%B0%E4%BB%A3%E4%B8%BB%E4%B9%89/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E5%BD%92%E5%9B%A0%E4%B8%8E%E7%94%9F%E6%B4%BB%E5%8C%96%E8%BF%81%E7%A7%BB%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT放松训练：网络疑病相关自主神经调节**: 在个案干预中段，针对网络疑病诱发的自主神经高唤醒状态，系统引导来访者习得并练习渐进式肌肉放松或腹式呼吸技术，并通过结构化家庭作业巩固技能；全程强调正常化、非评判觉察与个体化调适。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%94%BE%E6%9D%BE%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83%EF%BC%9A%E7%BD%91%E7%BB%9C%E7%96%91%E7%97%85%E7%9B%B8%E5%85%B3%E8%87%AA%E4%B8%BB%E7%A5%9E%E7%BB%8F%E8%B0%83%E8%8A%82) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT暴露后恶性循环认知解构**: 在单次现实暴露结束后，引导来访者识别并命名由回避行为维持的‘焦虑-厌恶-回避’自我强化循环，用具体事件链说明其如何阻断自然习惯化过程。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%9A%B4%E9%9C%B2%E5%90%8E%E6%81%B6%E6%80%A7%E5%BE%AA%E7%8E%AF%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A7%A3%E6%9E%84) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念四句锚定小结**: 在单次CBT咨询结束阶段，以精炼、非说教、生活化方式陈述四句核心认知行为原理，用于强化概念内化、支持作业执行，并为后续干预建立共同理解框架。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E5%9B%9B%E5%8F%A5%E9%94%9A%E5%AE%9A%E5%B0%8F%E7%BB%93) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念巩固性重述**: 在巩固阶段，引导来访者用第一人称、现在时、肯定句式重述已修正的核心信念，强调接纳局限性与生活平衡价值，强化新认知的稳定性与自我归属感。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E5%B7%A9%E5%9B%BA%E6%80%A7%E9%87%8D%E8%BF%B0) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念探索与能力再评估工作流程**: 通过回顾个人经历、行为实验与能力盘点，引导来访者接触并松动关于自我、他人、世界的深层核心信念，重点重建自我效能感，适用于咨询中后期寻求根本性认同转变的情境。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E6%8E%A2%E7%B4%A2%E4%B8%8E%E8%83%BD%E5%8A%9B%E5%86%8D%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念温习微作业布置**: 在CBT单次咨询结束阶段，向求助者布置每日5分钟温习本次共同构建的1–3条合理核心信念的家庭作业，以强化神经可塑性、促进信念内化与行为迁移；严格限定形式（默读/朗读/录音复听）、时长（固定5分钟）、频次（每日一次）、起始时间（当日即始），不附加反思或实验任务。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E6%B8%A9%E4%B9%A0%E5%BE%AE%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%B8%83%E7%BD%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念矫正工作流程**: 通过识别自动思维→追溯中间信念→挖掘核心信念→检验证据→发展替代性核心信念的标准化序列，系统性更新来访者关于自我、他人、世界的核心信念。适用于已建立稳定咨询关系、能耐受认知探索的情绪困扰来访者。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E7%9F%AB%E6%AD%A3%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT核心信念识别与协同重构**: 在咨询初期、信任关系已初步建立的前提下，运用CBT认知概念化框架识别求助者不合理核心信念，并通过苏格拉底式提问、证据检验与协作生成等方式，引导其发展更具现实性、适应性与弹性的替代信念。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%A0%B8%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E5%8D%8F%E5%90%8C%E9%87%8D%E6%9E%84) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT深呼吸放松训练**: 引导来访者通过有节奏的深呼吸练习实现生理与心理同步放松，用于缓解急性焦虑、改善睡眠启动障碍。适用于咨询中或日常自我调节场景。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%B7%B1%E5%91%BC%E5%90%B8%E6%94%BE%E6%9D%BE%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT渐进式意象放松引导**: 在首次咨询中向来访者教授标准化渐进性放松训练步骤，指导其每日3分钟自主练习，并明确反馈要求，以快速缓解焦虑、改善睡眠及增强自我调节感。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E6%B8%90%E8%BF%9B%E5%BC%8F%E6%84%8F%E8%B1%A1%E6%94%BE%E6%9D%BE%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT焦虑-羞愧情绪链识别微干预**: 在第3–4次CBT咨询中，基于个案具体生理/行为线索（如手心出汗、回避检查），引导其即时识别羞愧、内疚、自责等特定情绪，并锚定其背后的自动化思维与中间信念，构建‘情境→情绪→思维→信念’的可命名、可复述的觉察链条。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91-%E7%BE%9E%E6%84%A7%E6%83%85%E7%BB%AA%E9%93%BE%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E5%BE%AE%E5%B9%B2%E9%A2%84) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT焦虑干预四阶段流程**: 一个结构化、分阶段的CBT个案干预框架，适用于学习受挫引发的青少年焦虑问题，含评估、认知重构、行为练习与巩固四个阶段。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E5%B9%B2%E9%A2%84%E5%9B%9B%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT现实暴露中的实时情绪锚定与反馈**: 在可控现实暴露过程中，咨询师每3～5分钟主动询问来访者当前主观焦虑强度（0–10分），同步引导其觉察情绪峰值时刻的自动思维变化，并在暴露结束后即时联结情绪消退体验与认知修正（如‘所担心的事并未发生’），以强化情绪-认知联结学习。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%8E%B0%E5%AE%9E%E6%9A%B4%E9%9C%B2%E4%B8%AD%E7%9A%84%E5%AE%9E%E6%97%B6%E6%83%85%E7%BB%AA%E9%94%9A%E5%AE%9A%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%8D%E9%A6%88) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT现实暴露任务分级与实施计划**: 在认知行为疗法框架下，为焦虑相关人际情境设计等级化暴露任务，并协同来访者制定包含去灾难化、回避行为教育、目标设定三要素的暴露实施计划。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%8E%B0%E5%AE%9E%E6%9A%B4%E9%9C%B2%E4%BB%BB%E5%8A%A1%E5%88%86%E7%BA%A7%E4%B8%8E%E5%AE%9E%E6%96%BD%E8%AE%A1%E5%88%92) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT社交恐惧症暴露练习结构化方案**: 在认知行为疗法框架下，为社交恐怖症求助者设计并实施渐进式暴露练习的结构化会话流程，含情绪监控、情景分级、作业布置与反思要求。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%8A%A8%E5%8A%9B%E5%AD%A6/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%A4%BE%E4%BA%A4%E6%81%90%E6%83%A7%E7%97%87%E6%9A%B4%E9%9C%B2%E7%BB%83%E4%B9%A0%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%96%B9%E6%A1%88) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT社交焦虑阶段性咨询框架**: 一个结构化、分阶段的CBT咨询流程，覆盖从建立联盟、认知重构到行为暴露与复发预防的全过程，专用于社交焦虑障碍的系统干预。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%A4%BE%E4%BA%A4%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E6%80%A7%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT终期会谈结构化框架**: 在认知行为疗法（CBT）第10次（终期）咨询中，系统性完成认知成果巩固、疗效评估、现实迁移预演与分离情绪处理的标准化会谈流程。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%BB%88%E6%9C%9F%E4%BC%9A%E8%B0%88%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT结束阶段三重巩固协议**: 在最后一次CBT咨询中同步执行三项核心任务：引导来访者回顾自身变化与已掌握的有效技术、协助处理分离焦虑、共同构建现实可行的复发预防策略，形成结构化、内化导向的闭环收尾。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%BB%93%E6%9D%9F%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E4%B8%89%E9%87%8D%E5%B7%A9%E5%9B%BA%E5%8D%8F%E8%AE%AE) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT结束阶段再教育与技能泛化工作流程**: 在CBT咨询末期，结构化回顾咨询历程，强化新习得的认知与行为技能，指导求助者将所学迁移至日常生活场景，尤其聚焦人际交往中的情绪调节与沟通技巧应用。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%BB%93%E6%9D%9F%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%86%8D%E6%95%99%E8%82%B2%E4%B8%8E%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD%E6%B3%9B%E5%8C%96%E5%B7%A5%E4%BD%9C%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT结构化单次咨询流程**: 在每次认知行为治疗会谈中，按固定顺序执行六个环节：当前状况与心境检查、与上次会谈的联结、议程设置、家庭作业回顾、议程议题讨论与新作业布置、阶段总结；确保会谈聚焦、高效且具教学性。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%8D%95%E6%AC%A1%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT考前焦虑三阶段咨询框架**: 一个面向大学生考前焦虑的结构化CBT咨询流程，分为建立关系、心理帮扶（认知重构）、巩固应用三个阶段，每阶段有明确任务与方法指向，提供跨会话的阶段性CBT干预主干。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%80%83%E5%89%8D%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E4%B8%89%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT考试焦虑三阶段咨询框架**: 一个结构化、阶段化的认知行为疗法咨询流程，覆盖诊断评估与关系建立、心理帮助（含认知重构与放松训练）、结束与巩固三个核心阶段，适用于考试焦虑个案。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E4%B8%89%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT考试焦虑三阶段咨询流程**: 一个结构化、阶段化的认知行为疗法咨询流程，专用于医学生考试焦虑干预，覆盖关系建立与评估、认知重建与技能训练、效果巩固与结束三个阶段，共5次会谈。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%B8%80%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%80%83%E8%AF%95%E7%84%A6%E8%99%91%E4%B8%89%E9%98%B6%E6%AE%B5%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT自动思维反驳话术模板**: 提供一组结构化、可复用的自我反驳语句，用于挑战‘我不行’类核心负性自动思维，适用于咨询中引导来访者进行认知重构练习。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%87%AA%E5%8A%A8%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E5%8F%8D%E9%A9%B3%E8%AF%9D%E6%9C%AF%E6%A8%A1%E6%9D%BF) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT自动思维识别与反应训练**: 引导来访者使用标准认知工具识别并回应自动思维，以觉察功能不良认知和逻辑歪曲，从而减轻情绪症状。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%87%AA%E5%8A%A8%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E5%8F%8D%E5%BA%94%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT自动思维识别与苏格拉底式提问引导**: 在认知行为疗法框架下，引导来访者识别特定情境中的负性自动思维，并通过结构化苏格拉底式提问促使其评估思维的真实性与适应性。适用于来访者已建立基本咨询关系、能报告具体情境与情绪反应时。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%87%AA%E5%8A%A8%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E8%8B%8F%E6%A0%BC%E6%8B%89%E5%BA%95%E5%BC%8F%E6%8F%90%E9%97%AE%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT自动思维识别与调整四步法**: 在CBT会话中期干预阶段，引导来访者系统识别并挑战功能不良自动思维的结构化技术：①证据检验；②生成替代解释；③评估可能性（最坏/最好/最可能）；④自我同情提问。聚焦思维可塑性与认知弹性培养，强调协作性检验与来访者主动生成。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%87%AA%E5%8A%A8%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E8%B0%83%E6%95%B4%E5%9B%9B%E6%AD%A5%E6%B3%95) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT自动思维识别与调整四步法**: 在CBT框架下，使用结构化提问序列引导来访者从情绪出发，逐层挖掘自动化思维、中间信念与核心信念，实现认知内容的外化与层级标注。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%87%AA%E5%8A%A8%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E8%B0%83%E6%95%B4%E5%9B%9B%E6%AD%A5%E6%B3%95-2) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT苏格拉底提问_不合理信念强度评估**: 使用0–100%百分比量化技术，评估来访者对已明确定义的自动思维（如‘我很失败’）的主观确信度，作为认知重构的客观锚点；聚焦强度而非真假，要求整数输出与简要依据，闭环执行。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%8B%8F%E6%A0%BC%E6%8B%89%E5%BA%95%E6%8F%90%E9%97%AE_%E4%B8%8D%E5%90%88%E7%90%86%E4%BF%A1%E5%BF%B5%E5%BC%BA%E5%BA%A6%E8%AF%84%E4%BC%B0) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT行为实验设计与实施**: 协同来访者设计可检验的假设性行为实验，用真实结果挑战适应不良信念，降低回避行为并提升情境自信。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E5%AE%9E%E9%AA%8C%E8%AE%BE%E8%AE%A1%E4%B8%8E%E5%AE%9E%E6%96%BD) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT行为日志家庭作业布置与结构化反馈**: 在首次咨询中标准化施测PHQ-9和GAD-7量表，向来访者解释结果含义，并据此形成初步工作评估，为CBT方案选择提供实证依据。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E6%97%A5%E5%BF%97%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E5%B8%83%E7%BD%AE%E4%B8%8E%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E5%8F%8D%E9%A6%88) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT认知修正引导框架**: 一个聚焦于早期教育与意义重构的CBT会话支架，用于帮助来访者理解症状成因、识别自动化思维，并发展替代性叙述，尤其适用于道德颓废、罪恶感与羞耻相关困扰。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E4%BF%AE%E6%AD%A3%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC%E6%A1%86%E6%9E%B6) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT认知偏差识别与重构引导**: 在咨询中期阶段，引导来访者识别失恋和家庭关系中的不合理信念，并通过共同分析促发其认知调整动机。适用于来访者已建立基本咨访关系、情绪初步稳定、能配合叙述具体场景时。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E4%BA%8C%E7%BA%A7%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E5%81%8F%E5%B7%AE%E8%AF%86%E5%88%AB%E4%B8%8E%E9%87%8D%E6%9E%84%E5%BC%95%E5%AF%BC) · ★620 · 3 copies)
- **CBT认知家庭作业结构化指派**: 在来访者已理解ABC模型并完成至少一轮认知质辩后，指导其在生活情境中实践结构化认知重构：记录事件→识别自动思维→标注认知扭曲→生成合理替代想法→应用自我暗示，形成闭环练习。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/CBT%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E5%AE%B6%E5%BA%AD%E4%BD%9C%E4%B8%9A%E7%BB%93%E6%9E%84%E5%8C%96%E6%8C%87%E6%B4%BE) · ★620 · 3 copies)

- **cc-lock-check**: cc-lock — soi trạng thái khoá của một file (không cố giành). Cho biết file đang trống, của mình, hay do clone khác giữ + owner. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-check))
- **cc-lock-coordination**: Use BEFORE editing files that other sessions may also touch (shared/high-traffic files, config, anything multiple branches change), AND whenever a cc-lock PreToolUse DENY appears (message "🔒 … is held by …", or offline-deny / fail-closed, or a STALE-BASE freshness DENY). cc-lock serializes concurrent Claude Code sessions across clones/worktrees via a shared lock-repo. On a DENY the correct move is to WAIT and retry, never to abandon the task. Triggers — cc-lock, DENY, "is held by", "đang bị giữ", file lock, multi-session, lock collision, parallel agents, stale-base, fresh-base guard. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-coordination))
- **cc-lock-fresh**: cc-lock — soi file có stale so với mainline không (probe freshness). Cho biết file có bị đổi trên mainline sau điểm rẽ nhánh hiện tại không, kèm mainline ref và commit gây stale. Hoạt động kể cả khi freshnessMode của repo đang là "off". ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-fresh))
- **cc-lock-gc**: cc-lock — dọn các khoá đã hết hạn (stale) dưới project trên lock-repo. Chỉ xoá ref đã stale qua CAS, không đụng khoá còn sống. Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-gc))
- **cc-lock-init**: cc-lock — kiểm tra kết nối lock-repo, sinh clone-id, dựng local mirror cho clone hiện tại. Chạy sau khi điền cấu hình (thường /cc-lock-setup đã tự chạy). Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-init))
- **cc-lock-list**: cc-lock — liệt kê MỌI khoá đang giữ của project trên lock-repo (clone nào đang giữ file nào, hết hạn khi nào). Hỏi lock-repo qua mạng. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-list))
- **cc-lock-mine**: cc-lock — liệt kê các khoá mà clone hiện tại đang giữ (đọc cache local, không chạm mạng). Dùng để kiểm tra mình còn giữ file nào trước khi kết thúc. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-mine))
- **cc-lock-off**: cc-lock — TẮT cc-lock cho riêng clone/worktree này (ghi cc-lock-local.json). Các clone khác không ảnh hưởng. Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-off))
- **cc-lock-on**: cc-lock — BẬT cc-lock cho riêng clone/worktree này (ghi cc-lock-local.json). Không ảnh hưởng clone khác. Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-on))
- **cc-lock-release**: cc-lock — trả khoá cho một file (chỉ xoá ref nếu ta là chủ; luôn dọn cache). Gọi ngay khi đã xong một file để clone/session khác vào mà không phải đợi hết TTL. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-release))
- **cc-lock-release-all**: cc-lock — trả TẤT CẢ khoá mà clone hiện tại đang giữ. Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-release-all))
- **cc-lock-renew**: cc-lock — gia hạn (heartbeat thủ công) khoá đang giữ cho một file, đẩy expires_at ra xa. Thường hook tự renew nền; dùng khi cần gia hạn tay. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-renew))
- **cc-lock-setup**: cc-lock — thiết lập cc-lock cho repo hiện tại — điền lockRepoUrl vào cc-lock.config.json ở ROOT repo (projectKey mặc định "auto" — tự derive từ origin), rồi init + kiểm tra trạng thái. Lệnh do người dùng chủ động gọi khi lần đầu bật cc-lock cho một dự án. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-setup))
- **cc-lock-status**: cc-lock — báo trạng thái khoá cho repo hiện tại (active / enabled + nguồn / lock-repo / projectKey / clone-id). Dùng để chẩn đoán khi bị DENY hoặc kiểm tra cc-lock đã bật chưa. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-status))
- **cc-lock-wait**: cc-lock — xếp hàng chờ tới khi một file đang bị khoá rảnh rồi tự giành khoá. Dùng khi bị DENY và muốn tiếp tục sửa đúng file đó. KHÔNG BAO GIỜ huỷ task vì bị khoá — hãy chờ. ([TanTranDev/members-cc-lock](https://github.com/TanTranDev/members-cc-lock/tree/HEAD/skills/cc-lock-wait))
